# V4 tied-KV 稀疏注意力训练参考

仅一个 attention core 的去舍入数学图；包含 sink 梯度、共享 KV 两支路与重复 ID 归并。

官方 BF16 中间转换未复现；不是量化内核逐值等价、完整层训练或实际峰值。

| assumptions[0] | Real-arithmetic reference evaluated with FP64; source BF16 Q/KV/output and BF16 exponent cast before PV are not reproduced. No STE is asserted for that cast. |
| assumptions[1] | Softmax includes sink in stable max for real-math equivalence; source online max is over ordinary scores and adds sink only to denominator. Floating-point ordering differs. |
| assumptions[2] | Only -1 padding is supported; out-of-range IDs and all-invalid rows rejected. Repeated valid IDs remain separate probability entries with scatter-add gradients. |
| assumptions[3] | Matrix contractions use 2mnk including local outer products; scalar work never repeats their reductions. Reference scalar algorithm is declared, not Python helper instruction counts. |
| assumptions[4] | Valid logical slots only. Source 64-slot tile padding, wrapper head padding, online-softmax work and unknown backward launch strategy are outside this ledger. |
| assumptions[5] | The indexer training loss, compressor, projections, RMSNorm/RoPE, QAT and distributed communication remain outside this core. |
